Conclusion: 实验现象可通过莫尔超晶格中电荷预占引起的泡利阻塞机制成功解释，为原位调控极化激元非线性提供了有效途径。

Abstract: Transition metal dichalcogenides represent a versatile platform to study strong light-matter interactions based on excitons and electrons in ordered lattices. Twist-engineering of moiré structures further enables the manipulation of the polaritonic nonlinearities via engineering the exciton landscape on the nanoscale. In this work, we demonstrate in-situ control of the optical saturation-based nonlinearity of moiré exciton-polaritons by phase space restriction via charge doping. Strong exciton-photon coupling is established in a gate-controllable MoTe$_2$-MoSe$_2$ heterobilayer, embedded in a spectrally-tunable open cavity. A small gate voltage can effectively lower the necessary polariton density by one order of magnitude to achieve a similar nonlinear saturation effect as in the charge-neutral case. Our microscopic description successfully explains the observed phenomena in the framework of Pauli blocking for the moiré superlattices with charge preoccupation.

### [92] [Proximity effects of chiral magnetization on transition metal dichalcogenides monolayers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02241)

Abstract: We consider the proximity effects of a commensurate chiral $120^{\circ}$ Néel magnetic structure on the transport and optical properties of a transition metal dichalcogenide monolayer (TMD ML). The enlarged magnetic unit cell leads to the folding of the Brillouin zone and enables efficient intervalley spin-flip scattering. Starting from a six band tight binding model, we develop an effective $k\cdot p$ model, which describes the coupling between charge carriers in the TMD ML and the chiral magnetization. We predict the anomalous antiferromagnetic Hall effect (AHE) for conduction electrons and describe it accounting for the interplay between anomalous velocity, side jump, and skew scattering contributions. The proximity of the chiral magnetization also leads to the mixing and splitting of the exciton resonances in the two valleys which are related by the time reversal symmetry despite zero net magnetization. Finally, we demonstrate the possibility to measure the distribution of in-plane orientation of the chiral magnetization through Faraday rotation and the ellipticity of incident linearly polarized light.

Method: 理论推导RKKY相互作用在自旋-谷锁定中的等效Dzyaloshinskii-Moriya形式；采用平均场理论和经典蒙特卡罗模拟分析其对原子薄反铁磁体相图的影响。

Abstract: We put forward the Ruderman-Kittel-Kasuya-Yosida (RKKY) interaction in transition metal dichalcogenide monolayers as a tool to create and control a chiral magnetic texture. We show that in the spin-valley locking regime, the RKKY interaction acts as a Dzyaloshinskii-Moriya coupling with an effective spin rotation period exactly equal to the tripled lattice constant. Result: 在极低激光功率下实现了CrSBr磁序的局部和远程切换，可控制扩展横向畴域的磁构型，并能确定性制备零场磁状态。

Abstract: Manipulating magnetism with light is crucial for both fundamental understanding and technological advancements of information storage devices. The layered antiferromagnet CrSBr, part of the recently emerging class of two-dimensional van der Waals magnets, offers a unique path towards optical control of magnetism via magneto-excitons, which allow optical readout of the spin alignment and also provide a strong absorption channel. Here, we use exciton absorption and photoluminescence to demonstrate optical switching of the magnetic order in bi- and trilayer CrSBr. Using a continuous-wave laser with a power as low as a few microwatts at near-critical external magnetic fields, we demonstrate both local and remote switching of the magnetic configuration in extended lateral domains and further employ this mechanism to deterministically prepare the zero-field magnetic configuration. Our results establish optical switching as a useful means of controlling magnetism in CrSBr, with potential applications in magneto-optoelectronic devices.

Motivation: 探索可调控平带材料中非线性光学响应的增强机制，理解扭转角、偏压、费米能和堆垛构型对SHG的影响，并提出通用增强原理。

Method: 基于有效连续模型哈密顿量，采用微扰理论系统计算不同扭转角、垂直偏压、费米能及堆垛构型下的SHG响应，并分析态密度与共振条件。

Result: 小扭转角下SHG显著增强；除常规联合态密度机制外，平带窄带宽引发双共振过程，使ω和2ω跃迁在扩展的莫尔布里渊区同时满足，大幅放大SHG。AB–AB与AB–BA在强偏压下SHG存在系统性π相位移动。

Conclusion: 双共振效应是增强莫尔系统SHG的普适机制，为调控平带材料非线性光学响应提供了统一理论框架。

Abstract: We theoretically investigate second-harmonic generation (SHG) in twisted double bilayer graphene (TDBG) with AB--AB and AB--BA stacking configurations using a perturbative approach based on an effective continuum Hamiltonian. We present a systematic analysis of the SHG response as a function of twist angle, vertical bias voltage, Fermi energy, and stacking configuration. We find that the SHG signal is strongly enhanced at small twist angles due to the emergence of moiré flat bands and the associated increase in the joint density of states. Beyond this conventional enhancement mechanism, we demonstrate that the reduced bandwidth enables a pronounced double-resonant process, in which optical transitions at both $ω$ and $2ω$ are simultaneously satisfied over extended regions of the moiré Brillouin zone. This mechanism leads to a substantial amplification of the SHG response, analogous to the enhancement observed in systems with discrete energy levels, but realised here in a tuneable moiré band structure. Furthermore, we show that the AB--AB and AB--BA configurations exhibit a systematic $π$ phase shift in the SHG response at large bias voltages, reflecting their distinct symmetry and electronic structure. Our results identify double-resonance effects as a generic mechanism for enhancing SHG in moiré systems and provide a unified framework for understanding and controlling nonlinear optical responses in tuneable flat-band materials.

</details>


### [96] [Floquet spin-group framework and its application to light-tailored spin splitting in collinear magnets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02393)
*Tongshuai Zhu,Zixuan Li,Huaiqiang Wang,Su-Huai Wei,Jiawei Ruan*

Method: 将晶体自旋群与光的动力学群结合，建立统一Floquet自旋群框架，用于分析周期性驱动的、自旋轨道耦合可忽略的共线磁体，并据此分类不同驱动方案下自旋劈裂的宇称、动量依赖和节点结构。

Result: 该框架可系统决定光驱动下自旋劈裂的允许对称性；通过设计驱动场可在同一材料中切换奇宇称、偶宇称与混合宇称自旋劈裂，并在三维系统中发现高阶h波与k波自旋劈裂。

Conclusion: 该工作为光控自旋劈裂建立了统一对称性框架，为在交错磁体及相关磁性材料中工程化非平衡自旋能带结构提供了普适原则。

Abstract: Altermagnets combine momentum-dependent spin splitting with vanishing net magnetization, opening new opportunities for spintronics. Recent studies have shown that periodic driving by linearly or circularly polarized light, as well as by multicolor light fields, can control spin splitting in altermagnets and conventional antiferromagnets, and can even generate spin splitting patterns absent in equilibrium. Despite this progress, a unified principle connecting the dynamical symmetry of light to the symmetry of spin-split bands remains lacking, limiting the systematic design of light-induced spin structures. Here, by combining the spin group of crystals with the dynamical group of light, we establish a unified Floquet spin group framework for the study of periodically driven collinear magnets with negligible spin-orbit coupling. This framework systematically determines the allowed parity, momentum dependence, and nodal structure of spin splitting under different driving protocols. Guided by this symmetry classification, we show that odd-parity, even-parity, and mixed-parity spin splittings can be switched within the same material by tailoring the driving field. We further uncover higher-order $h$-wave and $k$-wave spin splittings in three-dimensional systems. Our work establishes a unified symmetry framework for light-controlled spin splitting and provides general principles for engineering nonequilibrium spin-band structures in altermagnets and related magnetic materials.

</details>


### [97] [Discrete states and ballistic interference in quantum wires approaching macroscopic lengths](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02431)
*Henok Weldeyesus,Taras Patlatiuk,Christian P. Scheller,Amir Yacoby,Loren N. Pfeiffer,Ken W. West,Dominik M. Zumbühl*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 在长达18微米的1D电子系统中，观测到离散量子能级和完全弹道的相干量子干涉；低偏压下可分辨长达10微米、填充100个电子的线中的轨道与自旋态，自旋填充严格交替，且干涉图样与无自由参数的单粒子模拟完全吻合，表明量子效应可接近宏观尺寸。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 增大量子系统尺寸受退相干、量子能级间距缩小及无序效应限制；1D系统因强横向约束增强量子效应，但对无序极为敏感。本文旨在展示合适的1D电子线在极长尺度下仍能保持理想量子行为。

Method: 利用两个平行量子线之间的隧穿谱，并引入中心栅极分段调制；搭建极低温低噪声测量系统；系统比较三种不同线长的干涉图样，并将实验结果与无自由参数的单粒子数值模拟进行对比。

Result: 观测到具有多个磁場和密度周期的复杂干涉图样，理论模拟无参数地复现全部周期；在低偏压下清晰分辨出库仑充电能、离散轨道态与自旋态（线长至10微米，100个电子）；自旋填充序列严格交替上下、无高自旋态，且峰电导随干涉图样调制。

Conclusion: 研究证明，在适当条件下，单粒子薛定谔量子力学描述的弹道量子干涉与离散量子态可在长达18微米、纵横比近1000的系统中被观测，展示了宏观尺度下保持量子相干的可能性。

Abstract: Increasing the size of a system showing quantum effects is a difficult task limited by decoherence, a diminishing quantum level spacing, and the effects of disorder spoiling the quantum behavior when growing in size. Systems in 1D offer very strong confinement in the transverse directions, thus generally enhancing quantum effects, but are notoriously sensitive to disorder. In this work, we present a system of 1D electrons exhibiting discrete quantum levels and fully ballistic coherent quantum interference with lengths of up to 18\,$μ$m. Tunneling spectroscopy between two parallel quantum wires with a central gated segment shows intricate interference patterns exhibiting several different periods in magnetic field and density. An analysis over three different wire lengths and a comparison with single particle numerical simulations without any free parameters remarkably explains the full pattern including the observed periods. Therefore, these wires are essentially ideal 1D systems with aspect ratios approaching 1'000. In addition, at low bias, we also observe not only the Coulomb charging energies but can clearly resolve the discrete orbital and spin states in up to 10\,$μ$m long wires when filling 100 electrons with the center gate. This is made visible by a state-of-the-art low temperature and low noise measurement system. The spin filling sequence is completely regular, strictly alternating spin up and down, avoiding high spin states, while the peak conductance is modulated in accordance with the previously discussed interference patterns. These striking results show that single particle Schrödinger quantum mechanics such as ballistic quantum interference and discrete quantum states may be observed, under the right conditions, in systems of up to 18\,$μ$m length, thus approaching macroscopic sizes.

</details>


### [98] [Comparison of Lindblad and circuit approaches for quantum heat transport](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02511)
*Bayan Karimi*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该文比较了量子电路中热微波光子热输运的两种模型：基于弱耦合Lindblad主方程（费米黄金规则跃迁率）的模型与基于电路Johnson-Nyquist噪声的Landauer型模型，发现二者在线性电路弱耦合极限下完全一致，并给出了典型腔中介导热输运电路的解析功率表达式，定量评估了弱耦合假设的适用范围。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子电路中热微波光子的热输运分析存在两种常用理论框架，但二者之间的关系与适用范围尚不明确，尤其是弱耦合Lindblad模型是否可靠。需要通过定量比较来验证其一致性并确定弱耦合假设的有效边界。

Method: 一方面从弱耦合Lindblad主方程出发，由热耗散源通过费米黄金规则得到跃迁速率；另一方面采用电路模型，将耗散元件的Johnson-Nyquist热噪声视为电流源，计算其在电路其他部分产生的焦耳功率，从而导出Landauer型热输运表达式（传输系数正比于电路跨导）。两者在一个腔中介两个热库的典型线性电路中进行对比，并推导解析功率表达式。

Result: 在线性电路弱耦合极限下，两种模型给出完全相同的结果；通过该比较获得了弱耦合假设在电路中的定量有效范围。

Conclusion: 由于两种模型结果一致，可以确信弱耦合Lindblad模型也适用于分析包含量子比特或非线性谐振子等元件的量子电路中的热输运。

Abstract: We compare two popular models applicable to analyzing heat transport by thermal microwave photons in quantum circuits. The first model is derived from a weak-coupling Lindblad master equation, with transition rates determined by Fermi's golden rule induced by thermal dissipation sources. The second approach employs a circuit model, where thermal Johnson-Nyquist noise generated by dissipative elements introduces currents, and consequently Joule power, in other parts of the circuit. This leads to a Landauer type expression of heat transport where the transmission coefficient is proportional to the transconductance in the circuit. We find that the two models yield identical results in a linear circuit in the weak coupling limit with an analytic expression of power in an archetypal circuit of a cavity mediating heat between two baths. Our analysis yields a quantitative assessment of the range of validity of the weak coupling assumption in a circuit. Due to the correspondence of the two results, we feel confident in applying the weak coupling Lindblad model also for analyzing heat transport in quantum circuits consisting, e.g. of qubits and/or non-linear resonators.

</details>


### [99] [Strain engineering of transient exciton diffusion in WSe2 monolayers at cryogenic temperatures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02513)
*Roberto Rosati,Mohammed Adel Aly Nouh,Robert Schmidt,Rudolf Bratschitsch,Ermin Malic*

TL;DR: 本文研究了应变对低温下hBN封装WSe2单层中非平衡激子时空动力学的影响，发现拉伸应变通过抑制亮-暗激子间的声子介导散射，增大热激子过剩能量并加速其瞬态扩散；压缩应变则通过应变驱动的M声子发射导致瞬态扩散的周期性调制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 钨基过渡金属二硫化物中暗激子为能量最低态，主导激子热化、传播及低温发光（声子边带）。应变可改变亮/暗激子能量差，但此前对应变如何影响瞬态光致发光和非平衡激子扩散的研究尚属空白。

Method: 实验上对hBN封装WSe2单层施加应变，在低温下测量瞬态光致发光和激子扩散的时空演化；理论上结合声子散射机制分析亮-暗激子布居转变及应变对激子过剩能量的调制。

Result: 拉伸应变显著提高热激子过剩能量，加速瞬态扩散，归因于亮到暗激子的声子介导散射被抑制；压缩应变下预测到由应变驱动的M声子发射引起的瞬态扩散周期性调制。

Conclusion: 应变可作为有效工具调控2D半导体的瞬态光致发光和激子扩散，为激子输运工程提供了微观机理层面的指导。

Abstract: Tungsten-based transition metal dichalcogenides exhibit dark excitons as the energetically lowest states. These are crucial for exciton thermalization and propagation and they dominate low-temperature photoluminescence via the emergence of pronounced phonon sidebands. After a resonant excitation, highly mobile hot dark excitons are formed, which quickly thermalize into an equilibrium distribution. The application of strain modifies the exciton energy landscape and, in particular, the relative energy separation between bright and dark exciton states. The impact of strain on the transient photoluminescence and diffusion of non-equilibrium excitons has remained largely unexplored so far. In this work, we investigate the spatiotemporal exciton dynamics in strained hBN-encapsulated WSe2 monolayers at cryogenic temperatures. We demonstrate that tensile strain abruptly increases the excess energy of hot excitons, thereby accelerating their transient diffusion. We trace this back to suppressed phonon-mediated scattering from bright to dark excitons. Furthermore, we predict a periodic modulation of the transient exciton diffusion in the presence of a compressive strain resulting from strain-driven emission of M phonons. The gained microscopic insights illustrate how strain can be used to engineer transient photoluminescence and exciton diffusion in technologically promising 2D semiconductors.

</details>


### [100] [Thermal Stability of Two-Dimensional Crystals with Extended OH Hydrogen-Bonded Chains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02544)
*Alexander V. Savin*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 通过数值模拟研究了六方氮化硼（h-BN）基底上多种含苯环和羟基的分子的单层结构动力学，发现它们能形成具有线性OH···OH···OH氢键链的稳定二维晶体，且具有高热稳定性；部分分子构成的h-BN多层结构可用于开发耐高温质子交换膜。

Method: 对h-BN单层上沉积的分子单层结构进行数值模拟，考察含苯环和羟基的分子（如苯酚、氢醌、对苯基苯酚、对乙酰氨基酚等）的二维晶体形成、氢键网络结构及热稳定性。

Abstract: Numerical simulations of the dynamics of monolayer structures of molecules deposited on a sheet of hexagonal boron nitride (h-BN) have been performed. It is shown that molecules containing benzene rings and hydroxyl groups in their structure can form stable two-dimensional crystals with linear chains of hydrogen bonds OH$\cdots$OH$\cdots$OH$\cdots$ Such structures are formed by the following molecules: phenol (C$_6$H$_5$OH), hydroquinone (C$_6$H$_4$(OH)$_2$), 4-phenylphenol (C$_6$H$_5$--C$_6$H$_4$OH), 4-(4-phenylphenyl)phenol (C$_6$H$_5$--C$_6$H$_4$--C$_6$H$_4$OH), paracetamol (CH$_3$C(O)NHC$_6$H$_4$OH), 4-hydroxybenzanilide (C$_6$H$_5$C(O)NHC$_6$H$_4$OH) and 4,4-dihydroxybenzanilide (C$_6$H$_4$OHC(O)NHC$_6$H$_4$OH). On the one hand, the benzene rings in these molecules ensure their strong interaction with the flat substrate; on the other hand, they do not hinder the formation of extended hydrogen-bonded chains. The monolayer structures of these molecules exhibit high thermal stability: the onset melting temperatures of their 2D crystals are 47, 187, 127, 247, 167, 307, and 377 $^\circ$C, respectively. The simulations allow us to conclude that multilayer structures composed of h-BN sheets and molecules of hydroquinone, paracetamol, and 4-hydroxybenzanilide can be used for the development of novel proton-exchange membranes capable of operating at elevated temperatures.

</details>


### [101] [Magnetic Skyrmion Interacting with Optical Skyrmion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02552)
*Lan Bo,Jian Chen,Xichao Zhang,Yan Zhou,Chengwei Qiu,Masahito Mochizuki*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文研究了单个磁斯格明子（MSk）与光学斯格明子（OSk）光束之间的相互作用，发现了旋转、跳跃和摆线三种非线性动力学模式，并揭示了光学驱动力各分量的作用机制以及三维传播相位（尤其是差分Gouy相位）对跳跃轨迹的调控。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 磁斯格明子和光学斯格明子分别在物质和光中体现拓扑性，但两者之间的相互作用尚未明确，探索二者耦合有助于统一拓扑粒子与拓扑场并发展新型光磁操控方式。

Method: 将光学驱动力分解为梯度力、轨道角动量力和自旋角动量力，分别对应径向约束、方位漂移和进动调制；通过分析MSk与OSk之间的磁化-极化耦合，解释跳跃运动的空间选择性，并在三维情形引入传播依赖相位（以差分Gouy相位主导）来表征轨迹变化。

Result: 识别出旋转、跳跃和摆线三种非线性动力学模式；跳跃运动源于OSk光束的方位不对称性，且具有由磁化-极化耦合决定的空间选择性；三维中耦合相位导致z不对称的跳跃轨迹。

Conclusion: 该研究将拓扑粒子与拓扑场统一在同一框架下，为实现螺旋度选择性和相位可编程的光磁子调控提供了新途径。

Abstract: Magnetic skyrmions (MSks) and optical skyrmions (OSks) embody topology in matter and in light, respectively. Here we investigate the interaction between a single MSk and an OSk beam. Three distinct nonlinear dynamical modes are identified: rotation, skipping, and trochoidal motion. By decomposing the optical driving force into gradient, orbital-angular-momentum, and spin-angular-momentum contributions, we clarify their respective roles of radial confinement, azimuthal drift, and precessional modulation. The skipping motion arises from the azimuthal asymmetry of the OSk beam and exhibits spatial selectivity originating from the magnetization-polarization coupling between the MSk and OSk. In three dimensions, the coupling acquires a propagation-dependent phase dominated by the differential Gouy phase, which yields $z$-asymmetric skipping trajectories. These results bridge topological particles and topological fields within a unified framework, offering helicity-selective and phase-programmable routes to optomagnonic control.

</details>


<div id='cond-mat.supr-con'></div>

# cond-mat.supr-con [[Back]](#toc)

### [102] [Analytical Charge Density Profile of Vortex Core in Weak-Coupling Superconductor](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00226)
*Chi-Ken Lu*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 自洽Bogoliubov-de Gennes计算表明，超导涡旋内部求解Poisson方程会将以单一符号为主的电荷耗尽转变为以π/k_F为周期、符号交替的电荷调制。本文通过Caroli-de Gennes-Matricon束缚态的解析处理，证明该现象源于涡旋缠绕去除一个Bessel通道、束缚态电荷不守恒导致扩展态补偿、以及大动量转移下屏蔽效率不足，最终留下被4k_F^2/(4k_F^2+k_TF^2)因子衰减的2k_F振荡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长期以来数值计算发现超导涡旋中的电荷密度并非简单耗尽，而是呈现符号交替的Friedel-like振荡，但缺乏直观的物理图像。作者希望给出一个初等解析解释，说明这种振荡不是精细效应，而是由电中性和有限波矢屏蔽失效共同决定的必然结果。

Method: 采用阶梯能隙（step-like gap）中的Caroli-de Gennes-Matricon束缚态，证明束缚态旋量的归一化几乎不依赖角动量，从而可将模和化为闭式。利用涡旋缠绕在核心内从完备性求和中移除一个Bessel通道，得到芯内密度为零并带有2k_F的Friedel振荡；芯外则得到1/r包络并在相干长度内衰减。通过要求束缚态电荷不积分到零而由扩展态精确补偿，分析长波完全补偿而费米圆直径处补偿失效，从而提取出振荡幅度的屏蔽修正因子。

Result: 芯内束缚态密度在涡旋线上为零，并携带波矢为2k_F的Friedel-like振荡；芯外密度呈1/r包络且衰减尺度为相干长度。束缚态电荷需由扩展态补偿，长波下完全抵消，但在费米圆直径附近失效，保留下的符号交替2k_F调制仅被因子4k_F^2/(4k_F^2+k_TF^2)削弱，该因子在任意金属中介于1/2与3/4之间，说明振荡并非脆弱效应。

Conclusion: 超导涡旋中符号交替的电荷振荡是电中性要求和屏蔽在大动量转移下效率低下的自然结果：在屏蔽后的总电荷密度中，平滑项相互抵消，只留下2k_F的波纹。该解析图像澄清了早期数值发现的物理根源，并揭示其普适性与稳定性。

Abstract: Self-consistent Bogoliubov-de Gennes calculations have long shown that solving the Poisson equation inside a superconducting vortex turns a one-signed charge depletion into a modulation that alternates in sign with period $π/k_F$. We give an elementary account of that result. Taking the Caroli-de Gennes-Matricon bound states in a step-like gap, we show that the normalization of the bound-state spinor is nearly independent of angular momentum, which collapses the mode sum into closed form. Inside the core the vortex winding removes one Bessel channel from a completeness sum, so the density vanishes on the vortex line and carries Friedel-like oscillations of wavevector $2k_F$; outside it the sum gives a $1/r$ envelope decaying over a coherence length, with a residual ripple. The bound-state charge does not integrate to zero, so neutrality obliges the extended states to compensate it exactly. That compensation is complete at long wavelength but fails at the diameter of the Fermi circle, and what survives is a sign-alternating $2k_F$ modulation reduced only by $4k_F^2/(4k_F^2+k_{TF}^2)$, a factor lying between one-half and three-quarters for any metal. The oscillation is therefore not a delicate effect but a consequence of neutrality and the inefficiency of screening at large momentum transfer: in the screened total the smooth terms cancel and only the ripple is left.

</details>


### [103] [Ferroelectric superconductivity in noncentrosymmetric metals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00338)
*Jason G. Kattan,T. Pereg-Barnea*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 该工作从微观层面研究了铁电金属中的超导电性，证明了极化涨落可以像传统BCS理论中的非极性声子一样诱导电子间的吸引相互作用，从而在某些晶格构型下实现超导与铁电的共存甚至由铁电性产生超导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期实验发现某些材料可同时表现超导和铁电，挑战了二者不相容或无关的传统观点；但理论上对铁电金属中二者共存机制尚缺乏微观理解。

Method: 采用离子极化场与巡游电子耦合的微观形式体系，允许自发极化均匀或空间调制；极化涨落作为电子间相互作用的媒介，类比传统BCS理论中的非极性声子，并基于简化BCS模型推导超导条件。

Result: 得出了超导与铁电共存甚至由铁电性诱导超导的具体条件，表明极化涨落能在特定晶格构型下提供有效吸引力并导致库珀配对。

Conclusion: 铁电性与超导电性并非必然不相容，极化涨落可作为配对中介，为理解铁电金属中非常规超导机制提供微观理论基础。

Abstract: It has recently been shown in experiments that certain materials can display both superconductivity and ferroelectricity, contrary to a long-standing conjecture that these two phenomena are incompatible, or at least unrelated. In this work we study superconductivity in ferroelectric metals, using a formalism of ionic polarization fields coupled to itinerant electrons, both of which are treated at the microscopic level. The ferroelectric order manifests as a spontaneous polarization that may be uniform or spatially modulated, and fluctuations of the polarization mediate interactions between the electrons. The polarization fluctuations give rise to attractive interactions that can lead to Cooper pairing in certain lattice configurations, analogous to the nonpolar phonons in conventional BCS theory. Working with a simplified BCS model, we derive conditions under which superconductivity can coexist with and even emerge from ferroelectricity.

</details>


### [104] [{\it Ab initio} prediction of $d_{x^2-y^2}$-wave superconductivity in infinite-layer nickelates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00512)
*Guang-Yu Guo,Ren-Guo Guo,Yun-Chen Liao,Yang-hao Chan*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 对最佳掺杂无限层镍酸盐 Re0.8Sr0.2NiO2 (Re=La, Pr, Nd) 进行了全ab initio超导理论研究，在同等水平处理电子-声子耦合、屏蔽库仑排斥和自旋涨落相互作用，发现其为双带、具有节点 d_{x^2-y^2}(±)-波间隙的超导体，且超导由自旋涨落驱动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无限层镍酸盐是潜在的非传统高Tc超导家族，但其超导配对机制和隙对称性仍存在激烈争议，需从第一性原理层面阐明其超导性质。

Method: 基于超导体密度泛函理论，对电子-声子耦合、屏蔽库仑排斥和自旋涨落相互作用进行同等水平的ab initio计算，分析费米面、Lindhard响应函数、超导临界温度、能隙结构及准粒子态密度。

Result: 发现 Re0.8Sr0.2NiO2 是双带超导体，在不同费米面口袋上具有符号反转的 d_{x^2-y^2}(±)-波间隙；关闭自旋涨落后 Tc 降至约0.01 K，证明其超导由自旋涨落驱动；自旋涨落在布里渊区中心的大准二维费米面口袋上比EPC和库仑排斥强一个量级，而EPC在角落小三维电子口袋上占主导；节点d波结构源于布里渊区角落Lindhard响应的显著峰；计算结果与多数实验一致，并给出了可验证的STM谱预测。

Conclusion: 无限层镍酸盐的超导配对机制为自旋涨落介导，具有非传统d波节点能隙；第一性原理方法能刻画其超导特性，并为进一步实验验证提供明确预测。

Abstract: Infinite-layer nickelates have recently emerged as a new family of potential unconventional high critical temperature ($T_c$) superconductors. However, fundamental questions such as their superconducting (SC) pairing mechanism and gap symmetry remain under intense debate. Here we present a fully {\it ab initio} theoretical study on the SC properties of optimally doped nickelates $Re$$_{0.8}$Sr$_{0.2}$NiO$_2$ ($Re=$ La, Pr, Nd), based on the density functional theory for superconductors calculations with electron-phonon coupling (EPC), screened Coulomb repulsion and spin fluctuation (SF) interaction treated on an equal footing. We find that $Re_{0.8}$Sr$_{0.2}$NiO$_2$ are two-band superconductors with sign reversal $d_{x^2-y^2}(\pm)$-wave gap functions on the different Fermi surface (FS) pockets. Interestingly, when the SF interaction is turned off, $T_c$ becomes negligibly small ($\sim$0.01 K), thus demonstrating that the superconductivity in $Re_{0.8}$Sr$_{0.2}$NiO$_2$ is driven by SF interaction. Moreover, our {\it ab initio} calculations reveal that the SF interaction is an order of magnitude stronger than both EPC and Coulomb repulsion on the large quasi-two-dimensional FS pocket around the Brillouin zone (BZ) center, thus leading to the SF-mediated pairing mechanism, although the EPC dominates on the small three-dimensional electron FS pockets at the BZ corners. The emergence of nodal $d_{x^2-y^2}(\pm)$-wave gap structure is traced to the pronounced peaks in the Lindhard response function at the BZ corners. Our calculated FS, SC critical temperature, nodal gap structure and SC quasiparticle density of states are consistent with most available experiments. Furthermore, predicted unconventional SC properties such as scanning tunneling spectra of La$_{0.8}$Sr$_{0.2}$NiO$_2$ and Pr$_{0.8}$Sr$_{0.2}$NiO$_2$ are ready for immediate experimental verifications.

</details>


### [105] [Evidence for superconductivity at 190 K in a pressure-overdoped cuprate](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00942)
*Alexander C. Mark,Huu T. Do,David Rodriguez,D. Gonzalez Arevalo,Adam Denchfield,Eduardo H. T. Poldi,Daniel P. Phelan,N. K. Man,Rssell J. Hemley*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 对Bi-2223超导体在高达60 GPa压力下的超导临界温度进行了系统研究，发现准静水压条件下T_c在25 GPa以下呈非单调变化，随后持续升高至60 GPa时达到190 K，超过目前报道的铜氧化物超导体临界温度，可能与高空穴掺杂下的第二个超导区域有关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 铜氧化物超导体的T_c可被压力调控，且压缩能够将空穴掺杂与化学掺杂解耦，从而在远超常压可实现的范围内获得过掺杂样品。为了探索更高T_c的可能性以及理解高掺杂区间的超导机制，需要在极端压力下系统研究T_c随压力的变化。

Method: 采用多种实验技术，在高达60 GPa的不同压缩环境中探测Pb0.4Bi1.6Sr2Ca2Cu3O10+δ（Bi-2223）样品中迈斯纳效应起始温度T_c随压力的变化，并对比准静水压与非静水压等压缩条件。

Result: 准静水压条件下，T_c在25 GPa以下表现出明显的非单调压力依赖（与先前报告一致）；随压力继续增加，T_c持续上升，在60 GPa时达到190 K，提供了超过迄今常压及高压下铜氧化物超导体报道值的证据。

Conclusion: 该结果支持高空穴掺杂区域存在第二个超导区域的提出，表明在极端压力下铜氧化物超导体的T_c可能被进一步推向更高值，为理解高掺杂超导机制及探索更高临界温度提供了新方向。

Abstract: It is well established that the critical temperature ($T_c$) of cuprate superconductors can be tuned by pressure. For example, compression decouples the hole doping from chemical doping allowing for overdoped samples far beyond what is possible at ambient pressure. In this work, multiple techniques are used to probe the onset of the Meissner effect at $T_c$ as a function of pressure in $\mathrm{Pb_{0.4}Bi_{1.6}Sr_2Ca_2Cu_3O_{10+δ}}$ (Bi-2223) to 60 GPa in different compression environments. Samples compressed under quasihydrostatic conditions exhibit a distinctive non-monotonic pressure dependence of $T_c$ below 25 GPa, in agreement with previous reports. With further increase in pressure $T_c$ climbs continuously to 190 K at 60 GPa. Evidence for critical temperatures exceeding those reported to date for cuprates at ambient and high pressures, the results may be understood in terms of the proposed second superconducting regime at high hole doping.

</details>


### [106] [Anisotropic transport of Josephson vortices in atomic-layer superconductors on vicinal surfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01196)
*Wenxuan Qian,Yash Chauhan,Ryohei Nemoto,Keisuke Sagisaka,Shunsuke Yoshizawa,Takashi Uchihashi*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 通过扫描隧道显微镜在具有邻晶面的原子层超导体Si(111)-(√7×√3)-In中直接观察到位错处的约瑟夫森涡旋，并测量其输运性质，发现电阻在面外磁场下随温度降低出现陡降，且表现出与原子台阶方向相关的强各向异性（方块电阻各向异性达10^3量级）。高温柔弱场区域为热激活蠕变，各向异性激活能；增大磁场使激活能各向异性抑制，在0.10–0.20 T实现一维无钉扎涡旋流动；极低温下涡旋运动由量子隧穿主导。基于这些结果构建的B-T相图展示了多个方向依赖的涡旋输运机制区域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原子台阶对表面二维超导体的性质有强烈影响，磁场下在台阶处形成的约瑟夫森涡旋可能主导低温输运现象，但此前缺乏实验验证。本研究旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 利用具有邻晶面的原子层超导体Si(111)-(√7×√3)-In，通过扫描隧道显微镜直接观察约瑟夫森涡旋，并在面外磁场下测量电阻随温度的变化，分析涡旋输运的各向异性及其与磁场、温度的关系。

Result: 观察到电阻随温度降低的陡降，其各向异性与台阶方向相关；方块电阻各向异性约为10^3。高温低磁场区涡旋呈热激活蠕变且激活能各向异性；增加磁场使激活能各向异性地降至零，出现一维无钉扎涡旋流（0.10–0.20 T）；极低温下涡旋运动由量子隧穿控制。据此构建了包含多个区域且输运机制方向依赖的B-T相图。

Conclusion: 实验证实了原子台阶处约瑟夫森涡旋的存在及其对输运的显著影响，揭示了方向依赖的涡旋输运机制，为理解原子层超导体中台阶诱导的涡旋物理提供了直接证据。

Abstract: Atomic steps have strong influences on surface two-dimensional superconductors. Josephson vortices formed at the atomic steps under magnetic fields may dominate transport phenomena at low temperatures, but its experimental verification is still lacking. Here, we report the vortex transport properties of atomic-layer superconductor Si(111)-$(\sqrt{7}\times\sqrt{3})$-In with vicinal surfaces, for which Josephson vortices are directly observed by scanning tunneling microscopy. A sharp drop in resistance with decreasing temperature $T$, detected under out-of-plane magnetic field $B$, reveals a distinctive anisotropy with respect to the atomic step direction. The anisotropy of sheet resistance, proportional to that of vortex mobility, amounts to the order of $10^3$ at intermediate magnetic fields. In the high-$T$ and low-$B$ region, Josephson vortices exhibit thermally excited creep motions with anisotropic activation energy $U_\mathrm{act}$. A further increase in $B$ suppresses $U_\mathrm{act}$ toward zero anisotropically, resulting in one-dimensional pinning-free vortex flow at $0.10 \lesssim B \lesssim 0.20$ T. At the lowest temperatures, the vortex motion is governed by quantum tunneling. A $B$-$T$ phase diagram constructed based on these measurements reveals multiple regions characterized by directionally dependent vortex-transport mechanisms.

</details>


### [107] [Bulk Ising superconductivity in an intercalated TaSe2 bilayer structure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01209)
*Yupeng Li,Zhaopeng Guo,Lihong Hu,Guoan Li,Siqi Wu,Xinyi Zheng,Xiao Deng,Zhiyuan Zhang,Anqi Wang,Xingchen Guo,Ziwei Dou,Peiling Li,Yuke Li,Fanming Qu,Guangtong Liu,Jin-Ke Bao,Guang-Han Cao,Li Lu,Jie Shen,Zhu-An Xu*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 通过合成非中心对称的插层2Hb-TaSe2双层，结合输运测量、能带计算、厚度依赖比值及超导二极管效应，提供了体态伊辛超导的多重证据，并揭示其随厚度演化的特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 体态伊辛自旋轨道耦合能便捷构建自旋轨道环境并催生奇异量子现象，但相关实验实现和材料证据仍然缺乏，亟需寻找并验证体态伊辛超导体。

Method: 合成插层2Hb-TaSe2双层结构，采用电阻率测量获取超导各向异性和面内上临界场，结合能带结构计算分析自旋劈裂与极化，并通过厚度依赖的Bc2‖/Bp比值和超导二极管效应进行交叉验证。

Result: 电阻率显示各向异性超导行为，面内上临界场显著超过泡利极限；能带计算呈现伴随面外自旋极化的能带劈裂；厚度依赖比值和超导二极管效应进一步支持体态伊辛超导，并显示其随厚度演化的额外特征。

Conclusion: 该研究为体态伊辛超导提供了坚实的实验与理论证据，并为在块体材料中寻找和设计伊辛超导体提供了重要参考。

Abstract: Ising spin-orbit coupling in bulk systems has drawn considerable interest for its ability to conveniently construct spin-orbit environments and enable exotic quantum phenomena. In this work, we synthesize intercalated 2Hb-TaSe$_2$ bilayers with noncentrosymmetric structure and, through multifaceted analysis, present multiple lines of evidence for the emergence of bulk Ising superconductivity. Resistivity measurements reveal anisotropic superconducting behavior, with a remarkably large in-plane upper critical field $B_{c2}^{\|}$ that exceeds the Pauli limit $B_{p}$. Band structure calculations further show band splitting accompanied by out-of-plane spin polarization. Collectively, these observations point to the presence of Ising superconductivity. Additional measurements of the thickness-dependent ratio $B_{c2}^{\|}$/$B_{p}$ and the superconducting diode effect not only further support the Ising superconducting nature of this material, but also reveal additional features of bulk Ising superconductivity evolving with thickness. Our findings provide valuable insights that may contribute to the search for bulk Ising superconductors.

</details>


### [108] [$d$-spacing distributions as a probe of nematoelastic response in iron-based superconductors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01264)
*Wenting Zhang,Ruixian Liu,Tingjun Zhang,Weiliang Yao,Xüe Fu,Hanqing Xie,Ziye Mo,Ting Guo,Kuo-Feng Tseng,Thomas Keller,Jitae T. Park,Fankang Li,Masaaki Matsuda,Avishek Maity,Long Tian,Pengcheng Dai,Xingye Lu*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 利用中子Larmor衍射测量多种铁基超导体的面内d间距分布宽度，发现未施加单轴应力时其在四方相随降温呈Curie-Weiss型展宽，可用平均场模型通过双线性向列-弹性耦合及空间变化对称性破缺应力解释；该展宽提供体敏感、补充电子与弹性测量的向列相关晶格响应探针，且Fe1.07Te行为不同。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 铁基超导体中电子向列性与正交晶格应变双线性耦合，使得向列关联能体现在晶格响应中。已有弹性电阻等测量主要反映电子或宏观弹性响应，需要体敏感的晶格探针来直接探测向列关联对晶格微观不均匀性的影响，并检验该现象是否普遍存在于不同掺杂类型的铁基超导体。

Method: 使用中子Larmor衍射测量温度依赖的相对d间距分布宽度（面内FWHM，ε_FWHM），研究对象包括电子掺杂Ba(Fe1-xCox)2As2、空穴掺杂Ba0.83K0.17Fe2As2、FeSe和Fe1.07Te；对比有无外加单轴应力两种情况，并用基于双线性向列-弹性耦合与空间变化对称性破缺应力的平均场模型解释观测到的Curie-Weiss型展宽。

Result: Ba(Fe1-xCox)2As2在无外加单轴应力时，四方相内ε_FWHM随降温以Curie-Weiss形式增大，拟合尺度T*随Co掺杂降低，其演化与弹性电阻推断的向列相图相似但响应函数不同；Ba0.83K0.17Fe2As2和FeSe也出现类似展宽；Fe1.07Te无外加应力时不显示延长的Curie-Weiss行为，而施加单轴压力则产生强烈各向异性展宽，可能包含场偏置晶格响应和非均匀加载贡献。

Conclusion: 中子Larmor衍射可灵敏探测与向列关联相关的晶格展宽，其Curie-Weiss型行为源于重正化的正交晶格柔度对空间变化对称性破缺应力的响应，是一种体敏感的、与电子和弹性测量互补的向列探测手段，并扩展了向列涨落对晶格影响的普适性认识。

Abstract: Electronic nematicity in iron-based superconductors (FeSCs) couples bilinearly to orthorhombic strain, allowing nematic correlations to appear in the lattice response. Here we use neutron Larmor diffraction to measure the temperature-dependent distribution of relative $d$ spacings in electron-doped Ba(Fe$_{1-x}$Co$_x$)$_2$As$_2$, hole-doped Ba$_{0.83}$K$_{0.17}$Fe$_2$As$_2$, FeSe, and Fe$_{1.07}$Te. In Ba(Fe$_{1-x}$Co$_x$)$_2$As$_2$ crystals without intentionally applied uniaxial stress, the in-plane distribution width, $\varepsilon_{\rm FWHM}$, increases on cooling in the tetragonal phase and can be described phenomenologically by a Curie--Weiss-like form. The fitted scale $T^*$ decreases with Co doping and evolves similarly to the nematic phase diagram inferred from elastoresistance, although the two experiments probe different response functions. Related broadening in Ba$_{0.83}$K$_{0.17}$Fe$_2$As$_2$ and FeSe supports extending this interpretation beyond electron-doped BaFe$_2$As$_2$. By contrast, Fe$_{1.07}$Te shows no extended Curie--Weiss-like regime without applied stress, whereas uniaxial pressure produces a strongly anisotropic broadening that can contain contributions from both the field-biased lattice response and inhomogeneous loading. A mean-field model with bilinear nematoelastic coupling and spatially varying symmetry-breaking stress explains the Curie--Weiss-like broadening in terms of the renormalized orthorhombic compliance. Neutron Larmor diffraction therefore provides a bulk-sensitive probe of nematic-related lattice broadening that complements electronic and elastic measurements.

</details>


### [109] [Electron-like high-temperature superconductivity induced by compressive strain in La2PrNi2O7 thin films](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01295)
*Zhiwei Wang,Zhengjie Wang,Huiyu Wang,Mingyi Zhu,Mengzu Shi,Zongyao Huang,Houpu Li,Shoucong Ning,Jing Tao,Tao Wu,Xianhui Chen*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 在双层镍酸盐外延薄膜中，通过臭氧辅助原子层外延实现极端压应变（-2.14%）下的高温超导（Tc_onset≈60 K），系统比较了应变与高压两种调控机制，发现二者虽均通过抑制自旋密度波诱发超导，但电子态本质不同：最优超导薄膜呈电子型（负霍尔系数），而高压晶体呈空穴型（正霍尔系数）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 验证外延压应变模拟高压效应的等价性，澄清应变与压力在调控双层镍酸盐超导电性中的异同，并探索更广泛的应变相图。

Method: 采用臭氧辅助原子层外延生长La2PrNi2O7薄膜，覆盖从-2.14%压应变到+0.91%张应变的连续应变区间，结合输运、磁化率及霍尔效应测量，并与高压晶体数据对比构建相图。

Result: 极端压应变（-2.14%）薄膜呈现Tc_onset=60 K、零电阻33 K、抗磁20 K及准二维超导特性；超导窗口在面外晶格参数上与高压晶体显著偏离，但面内参数一致；霍尔测量显示最优超导薄膜为电子型，而高压晶体和非超导张应变膜为空穴型。

Conclusion: 应变和压力虽非完全等效，但均通过调控关联强度（而非特定费米面拓扑）来驱动超导，该结果为研究镍酸盐多轨道物理及高温超导提供宏观平台。

Abstract: The realization of high-temperature superconductivity in bilayer nickelates under epitaxial compressive strain is widely interpreted as mimicking the effects of high hydrostatic pressure. To test the equivalence of these mechanisms, we investigated a comprehensive strain continuum ranging from compressive (-2.14%) to tensile (+0.91%). Crucially, via ozone-assisted atomic-layer epitaxy, we realized high-temperature superconductivity in as-grown La2PrNi2O7 films on NdAlO3 substrates, which induce the most extreme compressive strain in this material system. Under extreme compression (-2.14%), these films exhibit a Tc_onset of 60 K, zero resistance at 33 K, and a diamagnetic response at 20 K, with magnetotransport measurements confirming a quasi-two-dimensional superconducting nature. Comparing our phase diagram with reported data reveals distinct lattice responses: unlike in pressurized crystals, the superconducting window in epitaxial films diverges significantly in the out-of-plane parameter c (or c/ap ratio) but remains consistent with the bulk regarding the in-plane parameter ap. Crucially, while superconductivity in both systems emerges from the suppression of spin-density waves (SDW), Hall measurements reveal a fundamental electronic dichotomy: optimal superconducting films are intrinsically electron-like (exhibiting a negative Hall coefficient), in stark contrast to the hole-like nature (positive Hall coefficient) of high-pressure bulk crystals and non-superconducting tensile films. Ultimately, both tuning strategies effectively modulate the underlying correlation landscape - the true driver of superconductivity - transcending the constraints of specific Fermi surface topologies. This work establishes a macroscopic platform for probing the multi-orbital physics of nickelates, offering a new dimension for investigating high-temperature superconductivity.

</details>


### [110] [Spin Josephson diode effect induced by higher-harmonic spin Josephson currents in a diffusive Josephson junction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01991)
*Shin-ichi Hikino*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 在准经典格林函数框架下，理论研究了含Rashba金属层和铁磁交换场的扩散约瑟夫森结中的自旋约瑟夫森二极管效应，发现Rashba自旋轨道耦合与交换场共同作用产生有限的φ0相移，导致自旋电流的正反向不对称，从而实现无需外磁场的自旋约瑟夫森二极管效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索不依赖自旋单态抑制或外磁场即可实现非互易自旋输运的新机制，理解谐波分量之间的相互作用对自旋约瑟夫森二极管效应的影响。

Method: 基于准经典格林函数理论，解析推导了扩散约瑟夫森结中自旋约瑟夫森电流的一次和二次谐波表达式，并数值计算了自旋电流相移、效率随金属厚度和自旋轨道耦合强度的变化。

Result: Rashba自旋轨道耦合与交换场打破反演和时间反演对称性，在自旋电流-相位关系中引入额外余弦项和有限φ0相移，导致正向与反向自旋电流不对称，实现无外磁场的SJDE。效率随金属厚度增大而下降（因二次谐波被抑制），随自旋轨道耦合强度的变化则反映相移增强与谐波抑制的竞争效应。

Conclusion: 谐波分量的共同作用为无需抑制自旋单态关联即可实现非互易自旋输运提供了新机制，揭示了SJDE效率与材料参数之间的内在关联。

Abstract: We theoretically investigate the spin Josephson diode effect (SJDE) in a diffusive Josephson junction with a Rashba metal layer under a ferromagnetic exchange field. Within the quasiclassical Green's function framework, we derive analytical expressions for the first- and second-harmonic spin Josephson currents. The interplay between Rashba spin-orbit interaction and the exchange field breaks inversion and time-reversal symmetries, generating additional cosine terms in the spin current-phase relations and a finite phi0 phase shift. This phase shift induces an intrinsic asymmetry between forward and backward spin currents, leading to the SJDE without an external magnetic field. Numerical results show that the efficiency decreases with increasing metal thickness due to suppression of the second-harmonic component, while its dependence on spin-orbit interaction strength reflects competing effects between phase shift enhancement and harmonic suppression. These findings demonstrate that the interplay between harmonic components provides a mechanism for nonreciprocal spin transport without requiring suppression of spin-singlet correlations.

</details>


### [111] [Enhanced and robust superconductivity in La0.8Sr0.2NiO2 membranes compressed up to 210 GPa](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02042)
*Shu Cai,Yuqing Tian,Shengjun Yan,Jinyu Zhao,Bo Hao,Jianfeng Zhang,Shuaihang Sun,Yang Ding,Qi Wu,Ho-kwang Mao,I. Bozovic,Yuefeng Nie,Liling Sun*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 研究了自由站立La0.8Sr0.2NiO2膜在高达210 GPa超高压下的超导行为，发现超导转变温度呈穹顶状演化，峰值74.5 K在146 GPa，且超导在210 GPa仍存在，展现出前所未有的压力鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无限层镍酸盐薄膜的超导性开辟了新领域，但衬底限制了高压研究。先前自由站立Nd0.85Sr0.15NiO2膜在91 GPa下未见超导转变温度饱和，自然引发对更高压力下行为的探究。

Method: 制备自由站立La0.8Sr0.2NiO2膜，并施加最高达210 GPa的超高压，测量超导转变 onset 温度随压力的变化。

Result: 超导在210 GPa下依然存在；转变温度从常压的16 K升至146 GPa时的峰值74.5 K，随后在210 GPa降至57.4 K，呈现连续穹顶状压力依赖。

Conclusion: 这种在超高压下仍保持超导的鲁棒性在此前的高温氧化物超导体中未见，为理解镍酸盐超导机制及极端压力下的超导行为提供了新实验依据。

Abstract: The discovery of superconductivity in infinite-layer nickelate thin films has opened a new frontier for exploring unconventional oxide superconductors beyond the copper oxide family. However, the presence of substrate hampers investigations under very high pressure. Recently, this problem was circumvented by a study of freestanding Nd0.85Sr0.15NiO2 membranes, which revealed that superconducting transition temperature kept increasing as the pressure was ramped up to 91 GPa, without showing signs of saturation. Naturally, one wonders what would happen at even higher pressures. Here, we report that superconductivity in a freestanding La0.8Sr0.2NiO2 membrane persists under applied ultrahigh pressures up to 210 GPa. The superconducting transition onset temperature exhibits a continuous dome-like evolution, increasing from 16 K at ambient pressure to a peak of 74.5 K at 146 GPa, and then gradually decreasing to 57.4 K at 210 GPa. Such robustness of superconductivity against ultrahigh pressure has not been observed in high-Tc oxide superconductors before.

</details>


### [112] [Pressure-induced concurrent amorphization and superconductivity in topological material NbNiTe5](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02342)
*Lingxiao Zhao,Yi Zhao,Bangshuai Zhu,Qi Wang,Cuiying Pei,Juefei Wu,Jin-Ke Bao,Wen-He Jiao,Yanpeng Qi*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 对拓扑材料NbNiTe5在高压下的结构、电子性质及超导电性进行了系统研究，发现压力诱导的非单调正常态电阻演化、约10 GPa时非晶化伴随超导出现，且超导转变温度随无序增强缓慢升高至45.7 GPa时的1.4 K。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索过渡金属硫族化物中压力、结构无序与超导电性的关联，并扩展拓扑材料与超导交叉领域的研究。

Method: 结合高压电阻测量、X射线衍射和Raman光谱，系统表征NbNiTe5从0.7 GPa到45.7 GPa的结构与电子输运演化。

Result: 0.7–5.1 GPa内正常态电阻非单调变化并对应层间二阶结构转变；约10 GPa开始非晶化并出现超导，随后无序增强使超导转变更清晰，Tc从9.5 GPa的0.6 K缓慢升至45.7 GPa的1.4 K。

Conclusion: NbNiTe5在高压下成为过渡金属硫族化物超导家族新成员，其超导由结构无序调控，为理解无序体系中的超导机制提供了新视角。

Abstract: We have systematically studied the structural and electronic properties of a topological material NbNiTe5 under high pressure. The evolution of the normal state resistance shows a non-monotonic trend from 0.7 GPa to 5.1 GPa, in accordance with the second-order transition along the inter-layer direction observed in X-ray diffraction and Raman spectra. At around 10 GPa, the sample starts amorphization, which is concurrent with the emergence of superconductivity. Upon further compression, the structural disorder enhances and the superconducting transition becomes clearer, suggesting that the superconductivity is modulated by the degree of disorder in NbNiTe5 under high pressure. Within 45.7 GPa, the superconducting transition temperature (Tc) slowly rises from 0.6 K at 9.5 GPa to 1.4 K at 45.7 GPa. Our findings extend the family of transition metal chalcogenide superconductors and shed new light on understanding superconductivity in disordered systems.

</details>


### [113] [Cryogenic focused-ion-beam microstructuring enabling quantitative $c$-axis transport measurements in Tl$_2$Ba$_2$CuO$_{6+δ}$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02344)
*Ayanesh Maiti,Carsten Putzke,Linus Holeschovsky,Roemer D. H. Hinlopen,Chunyu Guo,Dorothee Herrmann,Seunghyun Khim,Berit H. Goodge,Andre W. Tyler,Michele S. Conroy,Andreas W. Rost,Andrew P. Mackenzie,Philip J. W. Moll*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 研究表明，传统聚焦离子束（FIB）加工会使过掺杂铜氧化物超导体Tl2201发生热驱动的氧流失，而低温FIB微加工能抑制这种损伤并保持从体相到原子尺度的晶体结构。利用该方法制备的微结构器件可定量重现面内电阻率和霍尔载流子密度，且测得的c轴绝对电阻率ρ_c(T)约为此前报道的三倍，使输运各向异性与已知费米面几何在各向同性弛豫时间近似下定量一致，解决了输运与量子振荡测量间的长期矛盾。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 相关量子材料中的绝对输运测量常受无序、不均匀性、几何不确定性和小晶体尺寸限制；FIB虽可制备几何精确的器件，但应用于铜氧化物超导体时存在离子束损伤问题，阻碍了可靠定量测量。

Method: 对比常规FIB与低温FIB微加工处理过掺杂Tl2201，评估氧流失与晶体结构完整性；利用低温FIB制备微结构器件，测量面内电阻率、霍尔载流子密度及c轴输运，并与既有数据和费米面几何比较。

Result: 低温FIB显著抑制热致氧流失，保护晶体结构；器件无需重新标定即可定量复现面内输运结果；c轴绝对电阻率约为此前报道的三倍，使输运各向异性与费米面几何在弛豫时间近似下定量吻合。

Conclusion: 低温FIB微加工是实现量子材料可靠定量输运测量的有效途径，尤其适用于受无序、不均匀性、几何不确定或小尺寸限制的体系；该工作解决了过掺杂Tl2201中长期存在的输运与量子振荡测量不一致问题。

Abstract: Absolute transport measurements in correlated quantum materials are often limited by disorder, inhomogeneity, geometric uncertainty, and small crystal size. Focused ion beam (FIB) technology offers a route to overcome many of these limitations by enabling transport devices with precisely defined geometry to be fabricated from lamellae extracted from carefully selected regions of a crystal, but its application to cuprate superconductors has been hindered by ion-beam-induced damage. Here we study the clean overdoped cuprate Tl2201 and show that conventional FIB processing causes thermally driven oxygen loss, while cryogenic FIB microstructuring largely suppresses this degradation and preserves the crystal structure from the bulk to the atomic scale. Microstructured devices quantitatively reproduce established in-plane resistivity and Hall carrier density measurements without rescaling. Applying this approach to $c$-axis transport, we obtain absolute $ρ_c(T)$ values approximately three times larger than previously reported, bringing the transport anisotropy into quantitative agreement with the known Fermi surface geometry within an isotropic relaxation-time approximation. These results resolve a long-standing discrepancy between transport and quantum oscillation measurements in overdoped Tl2201 and establish cryogenic FIB microstructuring as a route to reliable quantitative transport measurements in quantum materials where disorder, inhomogeneity, geometry, or small crystal size have previously limited experimental accuracy.

</details>


### [114] [Superconducting ternary compounds Li-X-B (X=Mo, W) within the mild pressure range: First-principles predictions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02362)
*Bangshuai Zhu,Juefei Wu,Dexi Shao,Junjie Wang,Yu Han,Cuiying Pei,Qi Wang,Jian Sun,Yanpeng Qi*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 本文通过第一性原理计算与晶体结构预测，沿三元组成线（TCLs）研究高压下Li-X-B（X=Mo,W）体系，预测多个三元化合物，发现Li2WB6和Li2WB4超导Tc约11K，且引入Li可将WB2的稳定压力从约100GPa降至0GPa，初步验证了TCLs策略的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超导氢化物通常需要极高压强才能稳定，三元化合物有可能降低稳定压强并保持超导性。为验证提出的三元组成线（TCLs）策略，选择Li-X-B（X=Mo,W）体系，旨在探索新型三元超导体、理解高压相图，并为三元化合物结构预测提供更高效的方法。

Method: 结合第一性原理计算和晶体结构预测，沿Li-W-B五条TCLs和Li-Mo-B四条TCLs进行系统搜索；利用凸包分析判断热力学稳定性；计算超导临界温度；并在0GPa下进行全三元搜索（FTS）以评估TCLs策略的可靠性。

Result: 预测出5个Li-W-B化合物和4个Li-Mo-B化合物；其中LiWB4、Li4MoB2、LiMo2B2在高压下热力学稳定，Li2WB6在0GPa时仅高出凸包0.02eV/atom，具有合成潜力；Li2WB6 P6/mmm和Li2WB4 R-3m超导Tc约为11K，与WB2 P6/mmm在约100GPa时的Tc相近；Li4MoB2 C2/m在压缩时出现Tc异常升高；FTS初步验证了TCLs策略的有效性。

Conclusion: Li原子的引入可将WB2所需的稳定压力从约100GPa降低至0GPa，为降低三元超导体合成压力提供了候选结构；TCLs策略在结构预测中初步得到验证，未来有望改进并促进三元化合物的高效探索。

Abstract: Among the superconducting hydrides under high pressure, a number of studies concentrate on the ternary compounds to explore unique superconductors, which are capable of reducing the stable pressure and maintain superconductivity. In this work, to verify our proposed strategy of ternary composition lines (TCLs) to explore ternary compounds, we combined the first-principles calculations and crystal structure predictions to study the ternary compounds Li-X-B (X=Mo, W) under high pressure. After calculations along five and four TCLs in Li-W-B and Li-Mo-B, respectively, five Li-W-B compounds and four Li-Mo-B compounds were predicted. The compositions of LiWB4, Li4MoB2 and LiMo2B2 could be thermodynamically stable under high pressure, and Li2WB6 is around 0.02 eV/atom above the convex hull at 0 GPa, which has potential for synthesizing. Both of the predicted Li2WB6 P6/mmm and Li2WB4 R-3m are superconducting and their Tc are around 11 K, which are similar to the Tc of WB2 P6/mmm around 100 GPa. An anomalous increase of Tc was found in Li4MoB2 C2/m upon compression. We carried out full ternary search (FTS) to evaluate the validity of the TCLs strategy in Li-W-B system at 0 GPa. Our results are helpful for understanding the phase diagram of Li-X-B (X=Mo, W) under high pressure and the introducing of Li atoms provide candidate structures to reduce the measured stable pressure from ~100 GPa in WB2 P6/mmm to 0 GPa. Meanwhile, we preliminary validate the strategy of TCLs in structure predictions and we expect to improve this strategy in the future, shedding light on the studies of ternary compounds.

</details>


### [115] [Emergence of Double-Dome Superconductivity in the Pressurized Dirac Semimetal BaMg2Bi2](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02394)
*Qi Wang,Juefei Wu,Cuiying Pei,Yi Zhao,Yiyan Wang,Yanpeng Qi*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 对狄拉克半金属BaMg2Bi2单晶的高压超导性质进行了系统研究，发现压力驱动的双穹顶超导行为，最高超导转变温度Tc分别为4.5 GPa时的6.67 K和10.4 GPa时的7.22 K。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 环境压力下BaMg2Bi2表现出与体相带拓扑共存的表面超导性，为理解拓扑材料中超导的起源与调控机制，有必要探究其在高压下的超导演变规律。

Method: 结合高压X射线衍射、霍尔电阻率测量和理论计算，系统表征了BaMg2Bi2在高压下的晶体结构、电子输运和超导性质。

Result: 实验发现压力驱动的双穹顶超导现象：第一个超导区域与压力调制的Lifshitz转变密切相关；第二个超导相则与从环境P3m1相到高压Pnma相的结构相变同时出现。

Conclusion: BaMg2Bi2中存在两种不同微观机制诱导的高压超导态，分别对应电子拓扑转变和结构相变，这为探索压力下拓扑材料中新颖超导态提供了新的实验依据和理论视角。

Abstract: Dirac semimetal BaMg2Bi2 is reported to be a unique topological material that manifests surface superconductivity that coexistswith bulk band topology at ambient pressure. Here, we present a comprehensive investigation of high-pressure superconductingproperties in BaMg2Bi2 single crystal. Significantly, a pressure-driven double-dome superconducting behavior was revealed, withthe superconducting transition temperature Tc approaching the maximum values of 6.67 K at 4.5 GPa and 7.22 K at 10.4 GPafor the first and second superconducting domes, respectively. The combination of high-pressure X-ray diffraction, Hall resistivitymeasurements, and theoretical calculations demonstrates that, the first superconducting regime is closely related to the pressure-modulated Lifshitz transition, whereas the second superconducting phase emerges concurrently with a structural transition fromthe ambient-pressure P3m1 phase to a high-pressure Pnma phase.

</details>


### [116] [Screening phonon-mediated superconductors from static orbital Hamiltonians](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02426)
*Jian-Feng Zhang,Ze-Feng Gao,Xiao-Qi Han,Dingshun Lv,Miao Gao,Kai Liu,Xinguo Ren,Zhong-Yi Lu,Tao Xiang*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 提出一种基于静态轨道哈密顿量的低成本框架，无需显式声子微扰计算即可识别强电子-声子耦合超导体，并在36,000余种化合物中发现34种动态稳定且计算Tc>10 K的候选材料，揭示两条高Tc路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 第一性原理超导体搜索受限于电子-声子耦合（EPC）计算的高昂成本，亟需一种低成本且物理透明的预筛选方法。

Method: 开发了直接从静态轨道哈密顿量评估EPC强度的框架，规避显式声子微扰计算；利用密度泛函微扰理论（DFPT）对代表性超导体进行验证，并应用于MattKeyBond数据库中超过36,000种化合物。

Result: 框架能以显著更低计算成本半定量刻画EPC尺度；验证出34个动态稳定、DFPT复核Tc>10 K的候选超导体，并识别出两种高Tc路径：金属化共价σ键路径（更有利于高Tc）与费米面态密度累积路径（增强Tc但幅度有限）。

Conclusion: 该框架为高通量超导材料筛选提供高效物理直观工具，且揭示了实现相对高Tc超导电性的主导机制，对未来超导体设计具有指导意义。

Abstract: The first-principles search for superconductors is severely limited by the high cost of electron-phonon coupling (EPC) calculations. Here we develop a low-cost, physically transparent framework that identifies strong-EPC materials directly from static orbital-based Hamiltonians without explicit phonon perturbation calculations. Verification using density functional perturbation theory (DFPT) for representative superconductors shows that the framework captures semi-quantitatively the EPC scale at substantially lower computational cost. Applied to more than 36,000 compounds in the MattKeyBond database, it identifies 34 dynamically stable superconducting candidates with calculated $T_c > 10$ K after DFPT verification. These candidates reveal two distinct routes to relatively high-$T_c$ superconductivity: a metallized covalent $σ$-bond route that is more favorable for achieving high-$T_c$ superconductors, and a Fermi-level density-of-states accumulation route that can enhance $T_c$ but usually to a more limited extent.

</details>


### [117] [Composite quantum geometry of superconductors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02434)
*Florian Simon,Thomas Bernat,Annica M. Black-Schaffer*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 本文研究超导态本身的Bogoliubov-de Gennes量子几何，证明在超导适配、配对轨道均匀性、正常态无自旋翻转三个条件下，BdG量子几何可严格分解为正常态量子几何与配对量子几何之和，并为自旋单态和三态（含非幺正三态）给出解析公式；在更一般的双轨道非均匀配对情形下，该分解失效，正常态与配对贡献相互交织。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期研究关注超导与正常态量子几何的关系，尤其是超流权重，但超导态自身的量子几何（由BdG哈密顿量决定）尚缺乏系统理解。作者希望厘清超导配对如何独立贡献量子几何与拓扑，从而为拓扑超导和量子度量调控提供设计规则，并辅助非常规超导的实验诊断。

Method: 基于BdG哈密顿量定义量子几何，系统考察自旋单态与三态配对；提出三个一般性条件（超导适配、配对轨道均匀、无正常态自旋翻转项），在此条件下证明量子几何的可加分解；对非均匀配对与有限超导适配的一般双轨道自旋单态超导体，推导显式解析结果，展示不可分离的复合结构。

Result: 在三个条件下，BdG量子几何严格等于正常态量子几何加上配对量子几何，且对所有自旋单态与三态配对（包括非幺正三态）均成立；配对量子几何具有显式解析公式，表明仅靠配对即可驱动拓扑和有限量子度量，即使正常态拓扑平庸或为平带。对一般双轨道自旋单态情形，正常态与配对贡献交织，形成不可分离的复合量子几何。

Conclusion: 这些结果为设计具有非平庸拓扑和有限量子度量的超导体及超导混合结构提供了直接规则，同时有助于非常规超导的识别和实验表征。

Abstract: The interplay of superconductivity and the quantum geometry of the normal state has recently been the subject of an array of studies, especially regarding the superfluid weight. In this work, we turn our attention to the quantum geometry of the superconducting state itself, set by the Bogoliubov-de Gennes (BdG) Hamiltonian, which dictates the geometric and topological properties of superconductivity. We show that under three general conditions, namely superconducting fitness, orbital uniformity of the superconducting pairing, and absence of normal-state spin-flip terms, the BdG quantum geometry exactly separates into a sum of the normal-state quantum geometry and an additional pairing quantum geometry, thereby displaying a simple composite structure. We show that this separation holds for all spin-singlet and -triplet pairings, including nonunitary spin-triplet pairing. We further provide explicit analytical formulas for the pairing quantum geometry for all these cases. These results establish how superconducting pairing alone easily drives both topology and a finite quantum metric, thus being present even in topological trivial or flat band superconductors, with no normal state quantum geometry. To complement these results, we also derive the BdG quantum geometry of a general two-orbital spin-singlet superconductor with non-uniform pairing and finite superconducting fitness. Here, our explicit analytical results establish a non-separable composite BdG quantum geometry, with the normal state and pairing contributions generally intertwining, thereby producing even more possibilities for finite quantum geometry. Our results provide design rules for creating superconductors and superconducting hybrid structures with nontrivial topology and finite quantum metric and will additionally help in the experimental diagnosis of unconventional superconductivity.

</details>


### [118] [Superconducting dome due to the Fano-Feshbach shape resonance in artificial high-Tc superlattices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02501)
*Gaetano Campi,Andrea Alimenti,Danielle Villa,G. Alexander Smith,Fedor F. Balakirev,Luis Balicas,Andrea Perali,Sergio Caprara,Gennady Logvenov,Antonio Bianconi1*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 通过分子束外延生长的人工高温超导SNSN超晶格，改变量子几何因子L/d调节化学势，在实验上验证了Bianconi-Perali-Valletta理论预言的超导穹顶，并在最高72 T脉冲磁场下系统研究了从欠掺杂到过掺杂的磁输运性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为Bianconi Perali Valletta理论提供令人信服的实验验证，该理论预言基于量子材料设计的人工高温超导超晶格会出现超导穹顶，因此需要制备特定纳米异质结构并通过调节量子几何因子来检验其超导行为。

Method: 采用分子束外延生长SNSN超晶格，其中S层为具有Rashba自旋轨道耦合的超导掺杂Mott绝缘体量子阱（厚度L），N层为金属间隔层，周期为d；通过改变L/d比值有效调节空穴浓度δ=0.45(1-L/d)和化学势，进而调控Fano-Feshbach形状共振；利用最高72 T的脉冲磁场对超导穹顶全掺杂范围（从深度欠掺杂到过掺杂）进行系统的磁输运测量。

Result: 实验获得了超导穹顶，并绘制了电阻转变、上临界磁场和Ginzburg-Landau相干长度随有效掺杂浓度演化的完整图像，证实通过L/d几何因子即可实现超导穹顶的量子设计。

Conclusion: 磁输运实验为BPV理论提供了有力支持，表明人工超晶格中L/d比值调控的Fano-Feshbach形状共振是超导穹顶出现的关键机制，为量子材料设计高温超导体提供了可行的实验途径。

Abstract: In this work we provide compelling experimental validation of the Bianconi Perali Valletta (BPV) theory predicting a superconducting dome based on a quantum material design of Artificial High TC Superlattices (AHTS) made with a selected nanoscale heterostructure geometry. These AHTS are SNSN superlattices of quantum wells of period d, composed of first units, superconducting doped Mott insulator layers with Rashba spin orbit coupling (S) of thickness L, intercalated by second units, normal metal spacers (N). In these superlattices, grown by molecular beam epitaxy (MBE), the experimental superconducting dome is obtained by material quantum design changing the chemical potential via the quantum geometrical factor L/d which tunes the Fano-Feshbach shape resonance in the pair transfer between superconducting gaps in the BCS regime and different gaps in the BEC-BCS crossover. Here we present a systematic magneto-transport study of AHTS artificial superlattices across the full doping range of the superconducting dome, from the deeply underdoped to the overdoped regime, using pulsed magnetic fields up to 72 T. By varying the L/d ratio, we tune the effective hole concentration delta=0.45(1-L/d) and map the evolution of the resistive transitions, the upper critical magnetic field and the Ginzburg-Landau coherence length

</details>


<div id='cond-mat.dis-nn'></div>

# cond-mat.dis-nn [[Back]](#toc)

### [119] [An asymptotically solvable model of many-body critical phases: mobility edges, scars, and inverted scars](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00157)
*Yi-Ting Tu,Zi-Jian Li,Sankar Das Sarma*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 构造了一个渐近可解的一维最近邻相互作用自旋链模型，其空间结构引发镜像式多体共振，在热力学极限下得到满足或违反弱本征态热化假说（ETH）的两相；两相可由有限温度相变（热力学多体迁移率边）分隔，并存在类似多体疤痕与反向疤痕的稀有本征态，同时对准周期MBL中雪崩不稳定性提出质疑。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随机多体局域（MBL）系统的预热 regime 主要由偶然多体共振主导；而在大型确定性系统中，另一类源于势能结构的共振预期存在，并可能导致单粒子临界相（既非局域也非扩展）。但其在相互作用系统中的后果尚不清楚。本文旨在研究这类结构诱导共振对相互作用系统的影响。

Method: 构造了一个一维最近邻相互作用自旋链的渐近可解模型，其空间结构产生层级式镜像多体共振，并通过热力学极限下的精确/渐近分析，检验一种弱本征态热化假说（ETH）版本的满足与违背，同时分析稀有本征态行为及相变性质，并延伸讨论相互作用Aubry-André模型中的罕见区域。

Result: 得到两个热力学相：一相满足弱ETH，另一相违背之，分别类似MBL相与ETH相；但每相都存在类似相反相行为的稀有本征态（多体疤痕与反向疤痕）。两相可由有限温度相变分隔，对应热力学多体迁移率边。此外，结果暗示在局域的相互作用Aubry-André模型中即使没有随机系统中的低无序区，仍可能存在非局域稀有区域，从而挑战准周期MBL无雪崩不稳定性的常见信念。

Conclusion: 结构诱导的镜像多体共振可在确定性相互作用系统中产生新的热化/局域化相变与稀有态，表明有限温度多体迁移率边并非不可能；同时为准周期系统中的MBL不稳定性提供了新的可能机制，需重新审视准周期MBL的稳定性判据。

Abstract: While the prethermal regime of random many-body localized (MBL) systems is dominated by accidental many-body resonances, another class of resonances, originating from the underlying potential structure, is expected in large-size deterministic systems. It is known that this class of resonances can lead to single-particle critical phases that are neither localized nor extended, but the consequences in interacting systems remain unclear. In this work, we construct an asymptotically solvable model of a one-dimensional nearest-neighbor interacting spin chain, whose spatial structure induces a hierarchy of mirror-like many-body resonances. We derive two phases in the thermodynamic limit, characterized by the satisfaction and violation of a version of the weak eigenstate thermalization hypothesis (ETH). While these two phases are similar to the usual MBL and ETH phases, there exist rare eigenstates that behave like the opposite phase, interpreted as many-body scars and inverted scars. Surprisingly, the two phases can be separated by a finite-temperature phase transition, corresponding to a thermodynamic many-body mobility edge, which was often believed to be impossible. Our results also suggest the existence of delocalized rare regions in an otherwise-localized interacting Aubry-André model, even if there are no low-disorder regions like those in random systems. This challenges the common belief that there is no avalanche instability in quasiperiodic MBL.

</details>


### [120] [Neural Quantum States for Nuclear Magnetic Resonance Spectroscopy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00178)
*Bharadwaj Chowdary Mummaneni,Bo Xing*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 本文基准测试了神经量子态（NQS）作为核磁共振（NMR）谱预测的变分表示，结合TDVP和p-tVMC传播方法，在2至5自旋分子上实现了高精度谱图，并通过相互作用帧传播大幅减少积分步数，成功扩展至14自旋的蔗糖分子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从第一性原理预测NMR谱需要传播维度为2^N的量子态，随着自旋数N增大而变得不可行，因此需要寻找替代的量子态表示方法来突破这一规模限制。

Method: 采用神经量子态（NQS）作为变分量子态表示，使用时间依赖变分原理（TDVP）和投影时间依赖变分蒙特卡洛（p-tVMC）两种传播方法，计算实验参数化分子的^1H谱；并提出在化学位移哈密顿量的相互作用帧中传播，以减少积分步数。

Result: TDVP在2至5自旋分子上重现全部谱线位置和强度，平均谱均方误差小于10^-3；p-tVMC也能重现相同特征，但精度取决于每步优化参数。相互作用帧传播在保持精度的同时，使3自旋系统积分步数减少约8倍，4和5自旋系统减少至少一个数量级，并实现了14自旋蔗糖分子的精确蒙特卡洛传播。

Conclusion: NQS结合相互作用帧传播为NMR谱预测提供了一种可扩展的替代方案，显著降低了计算成本，有望应用于更大自旋系统的第一性原理谱模拟。

Abstract: Predicting a nuclear magnetic resonance (NMR) spectrum from first principles requires propagating a quantum state of dimension $2^N$ for $N$ coupled spins, which becomes intractable beyond larger $N$. We benchmark Neural Quantum States (NQS), a class of variational quantum states expressed as an artificial neural network, as an alternative representation for this problem. Using two propagation methods, the Time-Dependent Variational Principle (TDVP) and projected time-dependent Variational Monte Carlo (p-tVMC), we compute the $^1$H spectra of four ($2 \to 5$ spins) experimentally parameterized molecules. TDVP reproduces all line positions and intensities with average spectral mean squared errors of $<10^{-3}$; p-tVMC reproduces the same features, with accuracy determined by its per-step optimization parameters. One dominant obstacle to larger systems is the steep growth of the number of integration steps with spectral bandwidth, which can be removed by propagating in the interaction frame of the chemical-shifted Hamiltonian. Retaining the same accuracy, this reduces the number of integration steps roughly eightfold for the 3-spin system and by at least an order of magnitude for the 4- and 5-spin systems, and it enables a 14-spin molecule (sucrose) to be accurately propagated via Monte Carlo sampling.

</details>


### [121] [Spin Quantum Hall Effect: the Critical Exponents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00849)
*Hrant Topchyan,Win Nuding,Andreas Klümper,Ara Sedrakyan*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 利用S-矩阵方法对自旋量子霍尔效应网络模型进行高精度数值计算，确定局域化长度临界指数ν和边界临界指数μ。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自旋量子霍尔效应（SQHE）是少数已知精确临界指数的安德森局域化相变实例，为无序拓扑系统与共形场论提供重要检验平台；其网络模型属于Altland-Zirnbauer C类，描述时间反演对称破缺的二维无序超导体中的准粒子输运，因此精确计算其临界指数具有基础理论意义。

Method: 采用近期发展的S-矩阵方法处理随机网络，对SQHE网络模型（由Chalker-Coddington模型中的U(1)随机相位替换为SU(2)随机矩阵得到）进行高精度数值模拟，分别提取体局域化长度指数ν和边界临界指数μ。

Result: 给出了局部化长度指数ν与边界临界指数μ的高精度数值结果，但摘要中未给出具体数值，仅说明为高精度计算所得。

Conclusion: 验证了S-矩阵方法在随机网络高精度临界指数计算中的有效性，并为SQHE相变及其与理论预测（如共形场论）的对比提供了可靠数值依据。

Abstract: The spin quantum Hall effect (SQHE) provides one of the few examples of an Anderson localization transition for which exact critical exponents are known, making it an important testing ground for theories of disordered topological systems and conformal field theory. The corresponding network model, obtained by replacing the random $U(1)$ phases of the Chalker--Coddington model with random $SU(2)$ matrices, belongs to symmetry class C of the Altland--Zirnbauer classification and is believed to describe quasiparticle transport in two-dimensional disordered superconductors with broken time-reversal symmetry. In this work, we perform high-precision numerical calculations of the localization-length exponent ($ν$) and the boundary critical exponent ($μ$) for the SQHE network model using the recently developed $S$-matrix approach to random networks.

</details>


### [122] [Predictive Formulas for Scattering Mean Free Path for General Disordered Dielectric Media Beyond the Long-Wavelength Regime](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01016)
*Jaeuk Kim,Salvatore Torquato*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 推导了d=1,2,3维统计均匀两相介质散射平均自由程ℓ_s的预测公式，适用于任意形状、多分散及非颗粒介质，仅需谱密度；在k_1/s≲1时与FDTD模拟及Mie理论一致，并预测超均匀介质的ℓ_s∼k_1^{-(d+1+α)}及隐身超均匀介质的透明窗口。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于Mie理论的估计仅适用于相同圆形或球形散射体，无法处理任意形状、多分散或非颗粒介质。需要一种仅由微观结构谱密度决定、普适且准确的散射平均自由程预测方法，以便理解和设计无序介质中的波输运。

Method: 基于有效动态介电常数的精确强对比展开，推导出d=1,2,3维统计均匀两相介质的散射平均自由程解析公式；微观结构通过谱密度进入公式。对五种非超均匀与超均匀模型应用公式，并用有限差分时域模拟验证。

Result: 当k_1/s≲1时预测与模拟吻合良好，且与适用范围内的Mie理论一致，并改进了二维横磁极化的精度；当k_1/s≳1时Mie估计更准确。对超均匀介质，理论给出ℓ_s∼k_1^{-(d+1+α)}；隐身超均匀介质在有限波数区间内透明。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了基于微观结构的通用预测与设计途径，可用于一般无序介电材料中波输运的调控与设计。

Abstract: We derive predictive formulas for the scattering mean free path $\ell_s$ of statistically homogeneous two-phase dielectric media in dimensions $d=1,2,3$. Unlike Mie-based estimates limited to identical circular or spherical scatterers, the formulas apply to arbitrarily shaped and polydisperse particulate media as well as nonparticulate media, with microstructure entering through the spectral density. The formulas are based on the exact strong-contrast expansion for the effective dynamic dielectric constant. We apply them to five nonhyperuniform and hyperuniform models and validate selected cases using finite-difference time-domain simulations. For $k_1/s \lesssim 1$, where $k_1$ is the incident wavenumber and $s$ is the specific surface, the predictions agree well with simulations and are consistent with Mie theory where applicable, while improving accuracy for two-dimensional transverse-magnetic polarization. Mie estimates become more accurate for $k_1/s \gtrsim 1$. For hyperuniform media with $\widetildeχ_V(k)\sim k^α$ at small $k$, the theory predicts $\ell_s\sim k_1^{-(d+1+α)}$; stealthy hyperuniform media are transparent over a finite wavenumber interval. These results provide a microstructure-based route to predict and design wave transport in general disordered dielectric materials.

</details>


### [123] [Griffiths phase in clique percolation in random geometric graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01487)
*Vasilii Tiselko,Olga Valba,Alexander Gorsky*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 本文研究了不同几何约束核下随机几何图系综中的团渗流，发现锐截止核存在宽Griffiths相（扩展临界性），幂律核表现出依赖团大小的有效ER与几何区域边界，指数核也进行了分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解几何约束对随机图团渗流临界行为的影响，特别是不同核函数是否导致非标准临界现象（如Griffiths相）。

Method: 通过理论分析随机几何图系综中团渗流，分别考虑锐截止、幂律和指数三种核函数，并识别渗流边界及相结构。

Result: 锐截止核下出现宽Griffiths相（功率律行为），边界分别对应ER渗流临界点和全局渗流点；幂律核具有更丰富行为，团大小依赖的ER/几何区域边界，且也存在Griffiths相；指数核模式也已分析。

Conclusion: 不同几何核决定团渗流相图，Griffiths相普遍存在，并对相关应用具有潜在意义。

Abstract: In this study, we discuss the clique percolation in the ensembles of random geometric graphs with different kernels that quantify the geometrical constraints. For the sharp cut-off we find the wide Griffiths phase of extended criticality with the power-law behavior. One boundary of the Griffiths phase is the generalization of a percolation critical point for the ER ensemble when the percolation within the large but finite cluster emerges. The second boundary corresponds to the point in the parameter space when the percolation in the entire clustered system becomes available. For the power-law kernel, richer behavior with a clique-size-dependent boundary between the effective ER and geometric regimes has been identified. The Griffiths phase in this case exists as well. Finally, the pattern with the exponential kernel has been analyzed. We briefly discuss the possible applications of our findings.

</details>


### [124] [Tunneling the Loss Landscape: Bypassing Memorization with Monte Carlo Parameter Swapping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01833)
*Lai Shun Chan,Xiaotian Zhang,Yue Shang,Ge Zhang,Entao Yang*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 本文通过参数流动性、副本相关性和分形维数三组分框架，直接验证了grokking动态与玻璃态弛豫的定量一致性，并提出受swap Monte Carlo启发的SAM-Swap优化插件以加速泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有理论将grokking类比为计算玻璃弛豫，认为快速冷却导致记忆化玻璃态、缓慢弛豫促成泛化，但该视角缺乏训练动态层面的直接实证验证。

Method: 提出三组分框架：参数流动性（PM）刻画参数更新能力，副本相关性（RC）和分形维数（FD）借鉴玻璃动力学；并在标准优化中测量这些指标，进而设计State-Aware Monte Carlo Parameter Swapping（SAM-Swap）优化插件。

Result: 标准优化表现出清晰的玻璃动力学特征：网络陷入动力学 arrested 的记忆化状态，具有低流动性、强历史依赖和通道状运动；SAM-Swap能加速泛化，且与权重衰减、高斯梯度噪声对比后发现，加速泛化始终与参数空间中的随机探索（类似物理扩散）相关。

Conclusion: 结果定量支持grokking作为玻璃弛豫的理论框架，并表明增强参数空间随机探索是加速泛化的有效机制，SAM-Swap可作为实用优化工具。

Abstract: Grokking is a striking phenomenon in neural network training, where a model can undergo a prolonged period of pure memorization before abrupt generalization. While previous works have attempted to interpret it through classical machine learning mechanisms like weight norm, recent research draws an analogy from statistical physics, framing grokking as a form of computational glass relaxation. This theory defines the initial memorization as a result of `fast cooling' where the training loss is reduced so quickly that a glass state is formed, followed by a `slow relaxation' towards final generalization. Although providing a unifying framework for representative grokking theories, this perspective has remained largely at the theoretical on macroscopic level without direct empirical validation on training dynamics. Here we introduce a three-component framework to directly characterize the training dynamics via parameter mobility (PM), and two representative measurements from glassy dynamics: replica correlation (RC) and fractal dimension (FD). We demonstrate that standard optimization presents clear signatures of glass dynamics and inherently traps the grokking network in a kinetic arrested memorization state with a collapsed mobility, strong history dependence, and channel-like motions. This quantitative agreement motivates us to introduce State-Aware Monte Carlo Parameter Swapping (SAM-Swap), an optimization plug-in that can accelerate generalization, inspired by swap Monte Carlo algorithm widely used in glass dynamics. Comparing SAM-Swap, weight decay, and Gaussian gradient noise, we find that accelerated generalization is consistently associated with random exploration in the parameter space, similar to diffusion in physics.

</details>


### [125] [Bayesian Inference with Structured Signal: Static Replica Symmetry Breaking on the Nishimori Line in the Planted Spin Glass](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02373)
*Andrea Vincenzo Dell'Abate,Louise Budzynski*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 本文通过随机正则图上的植入自旋玻璃模型，研究了信号先验中存在相关性（Ising耦合κ）对贝叶斯最优推断的影响，发现先验的顺磁、铁磁及自旋玻璃相分别导致重构阈值降低、先验本身支持部分恢复以及后验中出现静态复制对称破缺（RSB），并讨论了这对信念传播算法性能的后果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理论贝叶斯推断模型通常假设信号各分量独立同分布（i.i.d.），但真实信号往往具有相关性。为理解先验相关性对推断的影响，作者引入一个最小模型，将信号从具有耦合κ的Ising模型中采样，考察不同先验相下相关性如何改变推断的可行性与阈值。

Method: 采用随机正则图上的植入自旋玻璃模型，信号先验为Ising模型，观测通过典型通道生成。利用统计物理中复制方法、Nishimori条件以及信念传播（Belief Propagation）分析后验性质，分别考察先验处于顺磁、铁磁和复制对称破缺（RSB）相时的推断阈值、部分恢复能力和静态相变。

Result: 在顺磁相，信号相关性降低了重构阈值，使得较弱信号强度即可恢复；在铁磁相，先验本身就能实现部分恢复，并确定了观测提供额外信息的阈值；当先验处于RSB相时，在Nishimori条件下检测到后验中的静态RSB转变，首次展示了非可分离相关先验能在贝叶斯最优问题中导致静态RSB，且该玻璃相会影响信念传播的算法性能。

Conclusion: 先验相关性对推断的影响取决于其所在的相：顺磁相关有利，铁磁相关使先验起主导作用，而RSB相关则引入后验玻璃相，可能对信念传播等算法造成计算困难。这一结果为理解相关先验下的贝叶斯最优推断及算法极限提供了新见解。

Abstract: A common assumption in theoretical models of Bayesian inference is that the signal has i.i.d. components. To study the effect of correlations in the signal prior, we consider a minimal model: the planted spin glass on random regular graphs, where the signal is sampled from an Ising model with coupling $κ$. Depending on the phase of the prior, we find that adding structure in the signal can either help or hinder inference. In the paramagnetic regime, correlations in the signal lower the reconstruction threshold, so that weaker signal strength is sufficient for recovery. In the ferromagnetic regime, the prior alone already enables partial recovery, and we identify the threshold above which the observations provide additional information. When the prior itself is in a replica symmetry breaking (RSB) phase, we detect a static RSB transition in the posterior under Nishimori conditions. This provides an example where a non-separable, correlated prior leads to static RSB in a Bayes-optimal inference problem. We discuss the consequences of this glassy phase for algorithmic performance, in particular for Belief Propagation.

</details>
